# Classies Chronicles — Master Episode Generation Framework

**Proposed version:** CC-MEGF-0.03.00  
**Status:** Proposed update for review and approval  
**Scope:** Generic, AI-neutral framework for producing one daily chronicle at a time; ten chronicles are later manually compiled into one Classies Chronicles episode.

This update retains the original Edubeam, Koware, Eternal Dreamfall, Health & Safety, news and diary-driven concept, but reorganizes it into a repository-driven production system. It also aligns the episode with the Studio Structure Skeletal Blueprint’s principles: one persistent AI Desk, episode-owned archives, human editorial authority, provider neutrality, traceable retrieval, and controlled failure rather than invented content. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

***

## 1. Core production rules

1. **One Classies Chronicles episode spans ten working days.** It always begins on a Monday and ends on the Friday of the following week. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
2. **Only one chronicle is generated per working day.** The ten daily chronicles are collected, reviewed and manually edited into one complete episode; an AI must not generate all ten unless explicitly instructed. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
3. **The framework is generic.** It must not hard-code a date, episode number, destination, mission, mystery, character outcome or news event. Each generating AI must derive those details from the shared repositories, archives, endpoint data and continuity records. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
4. **Each chronicle must end with a challenge.** The challenge should appear at the end of the written chronicle and any comic or prompt-generated video, giving the audience time to work on it before the next chronicle reveals the answer. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
5. **The current day of the episode must be determined from the Millennium probe endpoint.** The endpoint supplies `phase_start_iso`, `phase_end_iso`, `now_iso` and `probe_iso`; the generating AI uses these to establish the active term, the first Monday of the term, the current episode day, and the Edubeam probe context. [buzzjuice](https://buzzjuice.net/shared/millennium-timezone/millennium-probe.php?probe=now)

### Calendar model

| Level | Structure |
|---|---|
| Year | One season |
| Season | Five chapters/phases/terms |
| Chapter/Phase/Term | Eight weeks, 40 days |
| Episode | Two weeks, ten working days |
| Chronicle | One working day |
| Weekly rhythm | Monday–Friday production; Saturday–Sunday are continuity days, not compulsory chronicle-production days  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |

The five terms run approximately: mid-January to mid-March; mid-March to mid-May; June and July; mid-August to mid-October; and mid-October to mid-December. The probe endpoint is the operational authority for identifying the active phase and its boundaries. [buzzjuice](https://buzzjuice.net/shared/millennium-timezone/millennium-probe.php?probe=now)

***

## 2. Source hierarchy

Each chronicle must consult the following sources in this order of authority:

| Source | Role |
|---|---|
| Classies Chronicles repository and Daily Diary | Established canon, prior chronicles, character continuity, personal notes and outstanding storylines.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| Eternal Dreamfall five-year Bible reading plan | Authoritative biblical reading sequence; do not invent daily allocations.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| YouVersion Verse of the Day | The actual verse for the publication day; verify reference, wording and translation.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| Buzzjuice Health & Safety courses | Verified practical safety lesson integrated into the story.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| Millennium Timezone and probe endpoint | Historical date, probe date, active phase boundaries and current episode-day determination.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| Koware Management repository | Business plans, projects, products, policies, roles, R&D context and organizational canon.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt) |
| Organizational chart with aliased checks | Character aliases, reporting relationships and the day’s featured-role schedule.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt) |
| Studio Structure Skeletal Blueprint | Production, AI Desk, archive, consent, media and publishing constraints.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/Studio_Structure_Skeletal_Blueprint/Studio_Structure_Skeletal_Blueprint_v22.2.txt) |

If a source is inaccessible, ambiguous, stale or conflicting, the chronicle must disclose the limitation or qualify the uncertainty. It must not fabricate Bible readings, verses, safety courses, organizational roles, historical facts, quotations or Edubeam capabilities. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

***

## 3. Episode narrative spine

Each episode should have one evolving storyline rather than ten disconnected daily scenes. The central situation must emerge from the archives, current Koware work, diary notes, selected headlines, Bible study and Health & Safety material—not from a default template. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

Recommended ten-day progression:

| Day | Narrative function |
|---|---|
| Day 1 — Monday | Establish setting, characters, organizational context, assignment and central storyline.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| Day 2 — Tuesday | Begin exploration, research or Edubeam deployment.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| Day 3 — Wednesday | Develop discoveries, relationships and complications.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| Day 4 — Thursday | Review progress, integrate findings and prepare next actions.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| Day 5 — Friday | Close the first week without prematurely resolving the episode.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| Day 6 — Monday | Re-establish continuity after the weekend and advance the assignment.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| Day 7 — Tuesday | Deepen investigation and character development.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| Day 8 — Wednesday | Converge storylines and expose consequences.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| Day 9 — Thursday | Reach the major decision, discovery or turning point.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |
| Day 10 — Friday | Conclude, report, hand over and establish continuity for the next episode.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |

The episode may end with a resolved assignment, a partial resolution, an unresolved continuation, or a handover to the next Edubeam team—but it must not force an artificial ending. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

***

## 4. Daily chronicle pipeline

Each daily chronicle should pass through the established transformation sequence:

1. **Eternal Dreamfall Bible Study** — identify the correct reading from the five-year plan and provide context. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
2. **Eternal Dreamfall Chronicle** — connect the Bible study to the YouVersion Verse of the Day while preserving the original storyline, motif and key elements. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
3. **Refreshed Eternal Dreamfall** — identify one relevant Health & Safety lesson from Buzzjuice and integrate it naturally. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
4. **Headlined Eternal Dreamfall** — integrate curated historical, current and probing-location headlines without distorting facts. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
5. **Classies Eternal Dreamfall** — integrate archives, Daily Diary notes and character continuity into an engaging narrative. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
6. **Classies Chronicles** — integrate Koware Group operations, organizational roles and the featured-role check schedule into the final daily chronicle. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

The final output must read as one coherent story, not six separate research sections. Biblical reflection, safety education, news, diary material and Koware activity should influence decisions, dialogue, incidents and mission outcomes. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

***

## 5. Episode-day and role scheduling

### Determining the active day

For every generation request, the AI must:

1. Call `https://buzzjuice.net/shared/millennium-timezone/millennium-probe.php?probe=now`.
2. Read `phase_start_iso` and `phase_end_iso`.
3. Identify the first Monday on or after `phase_start_iso`; that Monday is Day 1 of the term’s first episode.
4. Calculate the current date’s position within the active two-week episode.
5. Confirm that the current date is a Monday–Friday working day.
6. Use `now_iso` for current-world context and `probe_iso` for the Edubeam probe context. [buzzjuice](https://buzzjuice.net/shared/millennium-timezone/millennium-probe.php?probe=now)

If the current date falls on a Saturday or Sunday, no new daily chronicle is generated unless explicitly requested; weekend developments may be referenced in the following Monday’s chronicle. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

### Featured roles and check days

The organizational chart assigns aliases and check days to roles. The check-day notation means the episode day on which that role is placed in the spotlight; for example, `Day 11` means Day 1 of the next episode, and `Day 20` means Day 10 of the next episode. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

The updated framework should use this schedule as follows:

- Feature one or two principal roles per day; use up to four only when the story genuinely requires it. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Across ten days, rotate through enough roles to represent the Koware ecosystem without forcing every role into every episode. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- Use the role’s responsibilities, reporting line and alias from the organizational chart; do not invent positions or contradict established reporting relationships. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)
- Show how the featured role interacts with the Edubeam team, current Koware work, the day’s safety theme and the selected headlines. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
- The Corporate Manager/Founder’s routine remains flexible and diary-driven rather than fixed to one arrival or departure time. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

The current organizational chart provides a practical 20-day rotation covering stakeholders, administration, finance, legal/journalism, Corporate Manager and liaisons, creative/media, talent and grooming, enterprise architecture, R&D, human resources, health and safety, academic programmes, operations, facilities, public relations, marketing, international relations, and events/community outreach. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/Organizational_Chart_Structure-Aliased_Checks.txt)

***

## 6. Daily story structure

Each chronicle should be an immersive narrative of roughly three to five minutes, suitable for later conversion into comics, narration or short video. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

### Recommended chronicle sections

1. **Opening atmosphere** — early-morning environment, household, commute, workplace or Edubeam chamber, selected only where relevant.
2. **Spiritual anchor** — Eternal Dreamfall reading and Verse of the Day, woven into reflection or dialogue rather than presented as a detached sermon. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
3. **Health & Safety thread** — one practical lesson arising from the story, workplace, expedition or community situation. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
4. **News connection** — selected historical, current and probe-location headlines that meaningfully affect the mission. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
5. **Koware and role spotlight** — the day’s featured role, project or operational issue. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
6. **Edubeam mission development** — research, encounters, risks, discoveries and consequences, following established canon. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)
7. **Closing challenge** — a practical question, riddle, decision scenario or resource-based task, with the answer withheld until the next chronicle. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

### Challenge requirements

Every chronicle, comic and video should close with its challenge. The challenge should:

- Test practical understanding rather than simple recall.
- Connect to the day’s Bible passage, Health & Safety lesson, headline or Koware decision.
- Offer hints through service cards, riddles, library clues, treasure-hunt clues or related resources where appropriate.
- Have its answer revealed in the next chronicle.
- Support points, awards and leaderboard updates where the audience-participation system is active. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

***

## 7. Edubeam mission rules

The Edubeam remains the central fictional research and time-travel mechanism, but its behavior must follow established canon rather than being redefined by each AI. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

Each mission record should capture:

- Mission objective.
- Assigned personnel.
- Deployment location and temporal coordinates.
- Equipment, charge status and communication status.
- Discoveries, incidents and unresolved risks.
- Retrieval or return status.
- Links to prior missions and headlines. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

The framework should distinguish between a team preparing for deployment, actively deployed, continuing an assignment, missing a retrieval window, returning, undergoing decontamination, or preparing reports. A mission must not automatically restart each day. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

Established Edubeam constraints should be preserved, including the risks of large-object or long-range temporal transfer, communication distortion, unstable conditions, charge depletion, unauthorized information smuggling, and the need for controlled retrieval and post-mission checks. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

***

## 8. News, diary and continuity integration

### News

For each chronicle, curate headlines from:

- The historical date produced by the Millennium Timezone endpoint.
- The current date at the Koware headquarters location and globally.
- The probe date and probe location produced by the endpoint. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

Select a small, meaningful set of headlines—such as three global and five local items—then integrate them into the story only where they create a credible connection to the mission, safety lesson or organizational issue. Historical reporting must be accurate; future reporting must be clearly fictional speculation. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

### Daily Diary

Daily Diary notes provide personal context and narrative mirrors. A real observation may inspire a related fictional event in the Edubeam mission, but the AI must not present fictional events as real diary facts or force every diary note into the story. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

### Continuity record

Each chronicle should update a structured continuity record containing:

- Episode day and calendar context.
- Featured roles and aliases.
- Biblical, safety and headline references.
- Character developments.
- Mission status and temporal coordinates.
- New canon, proposed canon and unresolved questions.
- Source links and approval status. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

This record is essential when different AI systems generate different days. It should live in the episode archive, while the persistent CC_AI_DESK retains reusable intelligence infrastructure rather than becoming the permanent home of episode media or private material. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/Studio_Structure_Skeletal_Blueprint/Studio_Structure_Skeletal_Blueprint_v22.2.txt)

***

## 9. Studio and multimedia alignment

The written chronicle is the master narrative. Comics, images, narration, podcast segments and videos should be derived from the approved chronicle—not allowed to change the storyline independently. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

The production sequence should be:

1. Generate and review the daily chronicle.
2. Lock the approved narrative.
3. Produce comic panels, illustrations, narration or short video from that locked narrative.
4. End all reading, comic and video outputs with the chronicle challenge.
5. Archive the chronicle, sources, prompts, generated assets, approvals and continuity updates.
6. Compile the ten chronicles manually at the end of the episode. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles-Proposed_Episode_Framework_v0.01.05.txt)

The Studio blueprint’s content-multiplier approach can turn each approved chronicle into a full reading, audio narration, comic, short clips, social posts, Health & Safety material and course-promotion assets. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/Studio_Structure_Skeletal_Blueprint/Studio_Structure_Skeletal_Blueprint_v22.2.txt)

***

## 10. Editorial safeguards

The following rules are mandatory:

- Do not invent dates, readings, verses, quotations, courses, roles, news events or technical capabilities.
- Do not generate more than one chronicle per working day unless explicitly requested.
- Do not treat proposed fiction as established canon.
- Do not publish or promote unapproved generated media.
- Do not override consent, licensing or permission states.
- Do not silently change a character, mission, location or established storyline.
- Do not use inaccessible sources as a reason to fabricate content.
- Do not treat the AI Desk as the owner of episode-specific raw media or permanent episode knowledge. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

Where information is missing, the chronicle should state the gap, use only verified material, and preserve narrative uncertainty rather than inventing a resolution. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

***

## 11. Manual episode compilation

At the end of Day 10, the ten approved chronicles should be manually compiled through:

1. Chronological arrangement.
2. Character and mission continuity review.
3. Removal of repetition.
4. Transition smoothing.
5. Verification of biblical, safety, news and organizational references.
6. Confirmation that every instalment ends with its challenge and that answers are revealed in sequence.
7. Final editorial approval and archiving. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

The compiled episode should read as one continuous Classies Chronicles work while retaining the identity of its ten daily instalments. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)

***

## 12. Recommended supporting documents

To make the framework portable across ChatGPT, Claude, GitHub Copilot, Perplexity and future AI agents, maintain these reusable files:

| Document | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `CC-MEGF` | This master framework. |
| `Daily_Chronicle_Generation_Instructions` | Tells an AI how to research and produce exactly one chronicle. |
| `Episode_Continuity_Record` | Shared daily record of canon, missions, roles and unresolved threads. |
| `Source_Register` | Canonical repository, endpoint and archive links. |
| `Challenge_and_Answer_Log` | Tracks each daily challenge, hints, answers and participation points. |
| `Episode_Compilation_Template` | Supports manual editing of the ten chronicles into one episode. |
| `Post_Episode_Review_Template` | Captures lessons, continuity updates and improvements.  [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt) |

The governing principle is that the story belongs to the Classies Chronicles universe and its human editorial process—not to whichever AI generates a particular day’s chronicle. [raw.githubusercontent](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/cupidblack/Koware_Management/refs/heads/main/BlueCrown/Laboratory/development/classies_chronicles_episode_framework_proposal/Classies_Chronicles_Episode_Framework_compilation.txt)
